+def canonical_bytes(value):
+    return json.dumps(
+        value, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True

+def load_history(path, expected_sha):
+    raw = None
+    if os.path.isfile(path):
+        with open(path, "rb") as handle:
+            raw = handle.read()
+    observed_sha = sha256_bytes(raw) if raw is not None else "none"
+    if observed_sha != expected_sha:
+        raise RuntimeError(
+            f"sealed-history-mismatch: expected {expected_sha} observed {observed_sha}")
+    if raw is None:
+        return [], []
+    try:
+        _entries, phase4_runs, warnings = parse_history_document(
+            json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8")))
+        return phase4_runs, warnings
+    except (UnicodeError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
+        return [], [f"carry-forward skipped: history corrupt ({exc})"]
+
+
+def carry_forward(repo_root, phase4_runs, worktree_digest):
+    source = next((
+        record for record in reversed(phase4_runs)
+        if record["worktreeDigest"] != worktree_digest
+    ), None)
+    if source is None:
+        return None, "none"
+    files = []
+    for finding in sorted(
+            source["findings"],
+            key=lambda item: (SEVERITY_RANK[item["severity"]], item["file"])):
+        path = finding["file"]
+        if not all(char in SAFE_ASCII_PATH_CHARS or ord(char) >= 128
+                   for char in path):
+            continue
+        try:
+            path = validate_repo_path(repo_root, path)
+        except ValueError:
+            continue
+        files.append({"file": path, "severity": finding["severity"]})
+        if len(files) == 50:
+            break
+    if not files:
+        return None, "none"
+    value = {"files": files}
+    return value, "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(canonical_bytes(value)).hexdigest()
+
+

+PHASE4_ELIGIBILITY = {
+    ("full", "full", "completed"): True,
+    ("full", "full", "execution-failed"): False,
+    ("full", None, "skipped-full-run"): False,
+    ("full", None, "not-active"): False,
+    ("incremental", "diff", "completed"): False,
+    ("incremental", "diff", "execution-failed"): False,
+    ("incremental", None, "ref-invalid"): False,
+    ("incremental", None, "not-active"): False,

+def validate_phase4_contract(repo, manifest, phase4):
+    if not isinstance(phase4, dict):
+        raise Refused("phase4 evidence must be an object")
+    codex = phase4.get("codexReview")
+    if not isinstance(codex, dict):
+        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: codexReview must be an object")
+    if "state" not in codex or "promptVariant" not in codex or "carryForwardSha" not in codex:
+        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: required keys are missing")
+    state = codex["state"]
+    variant = codex["promptVariant"]
+    carry_sha = codex["carryForwardSha"]
+    if not isinstance(state, str):
+        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: state must be a string")
+    if state not in CODEX_REVIEW_STATES:
+        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: state is not recognized")
+    if variant not in ("full", "diff", None):
+        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: promptVariant is not recognized")
+    if not isinstance(carry_sha, str):
+        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: carryForwardSha must be a string")
+    key = (manifest.get("mode"), variant, state)
+    if key not in PHASE4_ELIGIBILITY:
+        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: mode, promptVariant, and state conflict")
+    normalized = set()
+    unresolved = 0
+    findings = phase4.get("findings", [])
+    if not isinstance(findings, list):
+        raise Refused("phase findings must be an array")
+    for finding in findings:
+        if not isinstance(finding, dict) or finding.get("source") != "codex-review":
+            continue
+        value = finding.get("file")
+        if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
+            raise Refused("codex-review finding file is missing")
+        severity = finding.get("severity")
+        if not isinstance(severity, str) or severity.upper() not in PHASE4_SEVERITIES:
+            raise Refused("codex-review finding severity is invalid")
+        try:
+            path = normalize_finding_path(repo, value)
+        except ValueError:
+            path = None
+        if path is None:
+            unresolved += 1
+        else:
+            normalized.add((path, severity.upper()))
+    rank = {"CRITICAL": 4, "HIGH": 3, "MEDIUM": 2, "LOW": 1}
+    ordered = [{"file": path, "severity": severity}
+               for path, severity in sorted(normalized,
+                                            key=lambda item: (-rank[item[1]], item[0]))]
+    return PHASE4_ELIGIBILITY[key], codex, ordered, unresolved
+
+
+def phase4_flip_count(records, current):
+    if current.get("truncated"):
+        return 0
+    keys = ("worktreeDigest", "contractVersion", "configSha", "carryForwardSha")
+    previous = next((record for record in reversed(records)
+                     if not record.get("truncated")
+                     and all(record.get(key) == current.get(key) for key in keys)), None)
+    if previous is None:
+        return 0
+    old = {item["file"] for item in previous["findings"]
+           if item["severity"] in {"CRITICAL", "HIGH"}}
+    new = {item["file"] for item in current["findings"]
+           if item["severity"] in {"CRITICAL", "HIGH"}}
+    return len(old.symmetric_difference(new))
+
+
+def trim_phase4_runs(records):
+    if len(records) <= 5:
+        return records
+    latest_digest = records[-1]["worktreeDigest"]
+    keep_indexes = set(range(max(0, len(records) - 5), len(records)))
+    source_index = next((index for index in range(len(records) - 2, -1, -1)
+                         if records[index]["worktreeDigest"] != latest_digest), None)
+    if source_index is not None:
+        keep_indexes.add(source_index)
+    return [record for index, record in enumerate(records) if index in keep_indexes]
+

+def validate_taint_evidence(value):
+    """Validate only identity/seal fields available immediately after open-run."""
+    if not isinstance(value, dict):
+        raise Refused("EVIDENCE is not an object")
+    if {"runid", "runDir", "lockIno", "config"} - set(value):
+        raise Refused("EVIDENCE taint identity fields are missing")

+    if (isinstance(value["lockIno"], bool) or not isinstance(value["lockIno"], int)
+            or value["lockIno"] <= 0):

+def rewrite_lock_holder(fd, holder):
+    raw = json_bytes(holder)
+    os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
+    os.ftruncate(fd, 0)
+    write_all(fd, raw)
+    os.fsync(fd)
+

+def quarantine_history(history_path, runid):
+    if not os.path.exists(history_path):
+        return
+    target = history_path + ".tainted-" + runid
+    try:
+        os.replace(history_path, target)
+    except OSError as exc:
+        raise HistoryQuarantineFailed("history-quarantine-failed") from exc
+    if os.path.exists(history_path):
+        raise HistoryQuarantineFailed("history-quarantine-failed")
+
+
+def taint_repo_from_run_dir(run_dir):
+    return os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(run_dir))))
+
+
+def early_taint(args):
+    run_dir = os.path.realpath(args.run_dir)
+    run_base = os.path.dirname(run_dir)
+    lock_path = os.path.join(run_base, "lock")
+    repo = os.path.realpath(args.repo_root) if args.repo_root else taint_repo_from_run_dir(run_dir)
+    state_dir = os.path.join(repo, ".claude", "state")
+    history_path = os.path.join(state_dir, "docaudit-history.json")
+    last_run_path = os.path.join(state_dir, "docaudit-last-run.json")
+    lock_fd = None
+    try:
+        expected = json.loads(args.expect_json)
+        validate_taint_evidence(expected)
+        if (expected.get("runid") != args.runid
+                or os.path.realpath(str(expected.get("runDir"))) != run_dir
+                or os.path.basename(run_dir) != args.runid):
+            raise Refused("EVIDENCE run identity mismatch")
+        lock_fd = os.open(lock_path, os.O_RDWR | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
+        try:
+            fcntl.flock(lock_fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
+        except BlockingIOError as exc:
+            raise Refused("lock is held by another process") from exc
+        lock_inode = os.fstat(lock_fd).st_ino
+        path_inode = os.stat(lock_path, follow_symlinks=False).st_ino
+        os.lseek(lock_fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
+        holder = parse_json(os.read(lock_fd, 65536), "lock")
+        if (not isinstance(holder, dict) or holder.get("runid") != args.runid

+            raise Refused("lock inode or runid mismatch")
+        if args.detail is not None:
+            if (len(args.detail) > 200 or any(ord(char) < 32 or ord(char) == 127
+                                              for char in args.detail)):
+                raise Refused("detail is invalid")
+        now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()
+        last_state = {"runid": args.runid, "verdict": "REFUSED",
+                      "reason": "config-changed" if args.taint_observed == "config"
+                      else "history-changed",
+                      "observedBy": args.observed_by, "ts": now,
+                      "reportStatus": "not-requested"}
+        if args.detail is not None:
+            last_state["detail"] = args.detail
+        if args.taint_observed == "config":
+            last_state["expectedConfigSha"] = expected["config"]
+            last_state["configAcceptanceRequired"] = True
+            atomic(last_run_path, last_state)

+            return 3

+        except HistoryQuarantineFailed:

+            holder_written = False

+                              "reason": reason, "observedBy": args.observed_by}, sort_keys=True))
+            return 3
+        atomic(last_run_path, last_state)
+        release_lock(lock_path, lock_inode)
+        print(json.dumps({"verdict": "REFUSED", "anchorWritten": False,
+                          "reason": "history-changed", "observedBy": args.observed_by},
+                         sort_keys=True))
+        return 3
+    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, Refused, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
+        print(json.dumps({"verdict": "REFUSED", "anchorWritten": False,
+                          "reason": str(exc)}, sort_keys=True))
+        return 3
+    finally:
+        if lock_fd is not None:
+            os.close(lock_fd)

+PHASE4_SEVERITIES = {"CRITICAL", "HIGH", "MEDIUM", "LOW"}
+PHASE4_RUN_FIELDS = {
+    "runid": str,
+    "ts": str,
+    "worktreeDigest": str,
+    "contractVersion": str,
+    "configSha": str,
+    "carryForwardSha": str,
+    "unresolvedFileCount": int,
+    "truncated": bool,
+    "findings": list,
+}
+PHASE4_RUN_LIMIT = 6
+PHASE4_FINDING_LIMIT = 500
+PHASE4_FILE_BYTES = 512
+PHASE4_RECORD_BYTES = 512 * 1024
+PHASE4_RUNS_BYTES = 1024 * 1024
 
 
 def sha256_bytes(data):
@@ -67,6 +86,80 @@ def parse_history(data):
     return entries
 
 
+def _canonical_size(value):
+    return len(json.dumps(
+        value, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True
+    ).encode("utf-8"))
+
+
+def _validate_phase4_path(path):
+    if not isinstance(path, str) or not path:
+        raise ValueError("finding file must be a non-empty string")
+    if (path.startswith(("./", "/", "//"))
+            or re.match(r"^[A-Za-z]:", path)
+            or "\\" in path or '"' in path
+            or any(unicodedata.category(char) == "Cc" for char in path)):
+        raise ValueError("finding file is not a canonical repository path")
+    if any(part in ("", ".", "..") for part in path.split("/")):
+        raise ValueError("finding file is not a canonical repository path")
+    if _canonical_size(path) > PHASE4_FILE_BYTES:
+        raise ValueError("finding file exceeds 512 serialized bytes")
+
+
+def _validate_phase4_runs(phase4_runs):
+    if not isinstance(phase4_runs, list):
+        raise ValueError("phase4Runs must be a list")
+    if len(phase4_runs) > PHASE4_RUN_LIMIT:
+        raise ValueError("phase4Runs exceeds 6 records")
+    if _canonical_size(phase4_runs) > PHASE4_RUNS_BYTES:
+        raise ValueError("phase4Runs exceeds 1 MiB")
+    for record_index, record in enumerate(phase4_runs):
+        if not isinstance(record, dict):
+            raise ValueError(f"phase4Runs record {record_index} is not an object")
+        for field, expected_type in PHASE4_RUN_FIELDS.items():
+            value = record.get(field)
+            if expected_type is int:
+                valid = isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)
+            else:
+                valid = isinstance(value, expected_type)
+            if not valid:
+                raise ValueError(
+                    f"phase4Runs record {record_index} has invalid {field}")
+        if _canonical_size(record) > PHASE4_RECORD_BYTES:
+            raise ValueError(
+                f"phase4Runs record {record_index} exceeds 512 KiB")
+        findings = record["findings"]
+        if len(findings) > PHASE4_FINDING_LIMIT:
+            raise ValueError(
+                f"phase4Runs record {record_index} exceeds 500 findings")
+        for finding_index, finding in enumerate(findings):
+            if not isinstance(finding, dict):
+                raise ValueError(
+                    f"phase4Runs record {record_index} finding {finding_index} is not an object")
+            _validate_phase4_path(finding.get("file"))
+            if finding.get("severity") not in PHASE4_SEVERITIES:
+                raise ValueError(
+                    f"phase4Runs record {record_index} finding {finding_index} has invalid severity")
+
+
+def parse_history_document(data):
+    """Return ``(entries, phase4_runs, warnings)`` for every valid history.
+
+    Invalid deterministic entries remain a corrupt-history error.  Invalid
+    Phase-4 sampling records degrade independently so deterministic cache data
+    remains usable.
+    """
+    entries = parse_history(data)
+    if isinstance(data, list) or "phase4Runs" not in data:
+        return entries, [], []
+    phase4_runs = data["phase4Runs"]
+    try:
+        _validate_phase4_runs(phase4_runs)
+    except ValueError as exc:
+        return entries, [], [f"phase4Runs ignored: {exc}"]
+    return entries, phase4_runs, []
+
+

                         contract_version, minimum, backend="workflow"):
     if not isinstance(backend, str) or backend not in VALID_BACKENDS:

+def normalize_finding_path(repo_root, value):
+    """Return a safe canonical finding path, or ``None`` if unresolved.
+
+    A trailing line/column suffix is removed only when the path including that
+    suffix is not itself a valid repository file.
+    """
+    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
+        return None
+    if (re.match(r"^[A-Za-z]:", value) or value.startswith(("/", "\\\\"))
+            or "\\" in value or '"' in value
+            or any(unicodedata.category(char) == "Cc" for char in value)
+            or len(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=True).encode("utf-8")) > 512):
+        return None
+    candidate = value[2:] if value.startswith("./") else value
+    try:
+        return validate_repo_path(repo_root, candidate)
+    except ValueError:
+        stripped = re.sub(r":\d+(?::\d+)?$", "", candidate)
+        if stripped == candidate:
+            return None
+        try:
+            return validate_repo_path(repo_root, stripped)
+        except ValueError:
+            return None
+
+

diff --git a/skills/audit/scripts/sealed_config.py b/skills/audit/scripts/sealed_config.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..a2604ef

+++ b/skills/audit/scripts/sealed_config.py
@@ -0,0 +1,107 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Read a JSON config once and verify its sealed SHA-256 before use."""
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+import re
+import sys
+
+
+SHA_RE = re.compile(r"sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}")
+_MISSING = object()
+
+
+class SealedConfigMismatch(Exception):
+    """The bytes read from config do not match the expected seal."""
+
+    def __init__(self, expected_sha, observed_sha):
+        super().__init__(expected_sha, observed_sha)
+        self.expected_sha = expected_sha
+        self.observed_sha = observed_sha
+
+    def __str__(self):
+        return (f"sealed-config-mismatch: expected {self.expected_sha} "
+                f"observed {self.observed_sha}")
+
+
+def load_sealed_config(path, expected_sha):
+    """Return ``(raw_bytes, parsed_json)`` after a one-read SHA check."""
+    if not isinstance(expected_sha, str) or not SHA_RE.fullmatch(expected_sha):
+        raise ValueError("expected config sha must be sha256:<64 lowercase hex>")
+    fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
+    try:
+        chunks = []
+        while True:
+            chunk = os.read(fd, 1024 * 1024)
+            if not chunk:
+                break
+            chunks.append(chunk)
+    finally:
+        os.close(fd)
+    raw = b"".join(chunks)
+    observed_sha = "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
+    if observed_sha != expected_sha:
+        raise SealedConfigMismatch(expected_sha, observed_sha)
+    return raw, json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
+
+
+def dotted_get(value, dotted_key):
+    parts = dotted_key.split(".")
+    if not dotted_key or any(not part for part in parts):
+        raise ValueError("--get requires a non-empty dotted key")
+    current = value
+    for part in parts:
+        if not isinstance(current, dict) or part not in current:
+            return _MISSING
+        current = current[part]
+    return current
+
+
+def main():
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    parser.add_argument("--config", required=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--expect-sha", required=True)
+    action = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
+    action.add_argument("--print", dest="print_config", action="store_true")
+    action.add_argument("--get")
+    parser.add_argument("--default")
+    parser.add_argument("--raw", action="store_true")
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+
+    try:
+        if not SHA_RE.fullmatch(args.expect_sha):
+            raise ValueError("--expect-sha must be sha256:<64 lowercase hex>")
+        if args.print_config and (args.default is not None or args.raw):
+            raise ValueError("--default and --raw require --get")
+        default = _MISSING if args.default is None else json.loads(args.default)
+        _raw, doc = load_sealed_config(args.config, args.expect_sha)
+        if args.print_config:
+            value = doc
+        else:
+            value = dotted_get(doc, args.get)
+            if value is _MISSING:
+                value = None if default is _MISSING else default
+        if args.raw:
+            if value is None:
+                output = ""
+            elif isinstance(value, str):
+                output = value
+            else:
+                raise ValueError("--raw value must be a string or null")
+            sys.stdout.buffer.write((output + "\n").encode("utf-8"))
+        else:
+            rendered = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=True, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
+            sys.stdout.buffer.write(rendered.encode("ascii"))
+        return 0
+    except SealedConfigMismatch as exc:
+        print(str(exc), file=sys.stderr)
+        return 7
+    except (OSError, UnicodeError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
+        print(f"sealed-config: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
+        return 2
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

@@ -22,7 +22,35 @@ Reads:  --config, --repo-root, --layer {format,existence,semantic,all},

-import argparse, json, os, re, sys, urllib.parse
+import argparse, hashlib, json, os, re, sys, urllib.parse
+
+
+class SealedConfigMismatch(Exception):
+    pass
+
+
+def load_config(path, expected_sha=None):
+    if expected_sha is None:
+        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as handle:
+            return json.load(handle)
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}", expected_sha):
+        raise ValueError("--expect-config-sha must be sha256:<64 lowercase hex>")
+    fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
+    try:
+        chunks = []
+        while True:
+            chunk = os.read(fd, 1024 * 1024)
+            if not chunk:
+                break
+            chunks.append(chunk)
+    finally:
+        os.close(fd)
+    raw = b"".join(chunks)
+    observed = "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
+    if observed != expected_sha:
+        raise SealedConfigMismatch(
+            f"sealed-config-mismatch: expected {expected_sha} observed {observed}")
+    return json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))

+Since v0.16.0, every plugin-engine config read is checked against the SHA sealed when the run opened. A `sealed-config-mismatch` stops the run and requires explicit `--accept-config` on the next open even if the config was restored. Phase-4 full review is sampling, not a convergence guarantee; gate-written history carries forward only validated `file` plus `severity` data and reports `phase4FlipsUnchangedContent` when blocking status changes under the same worktree, contract, config, and carry-forward inputs. Project-defined `docAuditCommands` remain trusted at repository-writer level.
+

   306	    ("full", "full", "completed"): True,
   307	    ("full", "full", "execution-failed"): False,
   308	    ("full", None, "skipped-full-run"): False,
   309	    ("full", None, "not-active"): False,
   310	    ("incremental", "diff", "completed"): False,
   311	    ("incremental", "diff", "execution-failed"): False,
   312	    ("incremental", None, "ref-invalid"): False,

   317	def validate_phase4_contract(repo, manifest, phase4):
   318	    if not isinstance(phase4, dict):
   319	        raise Refused("phase4 evidence must be an object")
   320	    codex = phase4.get("codexReview")
   321	    if not isinstance(codex, dict):
   322	        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: codexReview must be an object")
   323	    if "state" not in codex or "promptVariant" not in codex or "carryForwardSha" not in codex:
   324	        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: required keys are missing")
   325	    state = codex["state"]
   326	    variant = codex["promptVariant"]
   327	    carry_sha = codex["carryForwardSha"]
   328	    if not isinstance(state, str):
   329	        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: state must be a string")
   330	    if state not in CODEX_REVIEW_STATES:
   331	        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: state is not recognized")
   332	    if variant not in ("full", "diff", None):
   333	        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: promptVariant is not recognized")
   334	    if not isinstance(carry_sha, str):
   335	        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: carryForwardSha must be a string")
   336	    key = (manifest.get("mode"), variant, state)
   337	    if key not in PHASE4_ELIGIBILITY:
   338	        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: mode, promptVariant, and state conflict")
   339	    normalized = set()
   340	    unresolved = 0
   341	    findings = phase4.get("findings", [])
   342	    if not isinstance(findings, list):
   343	        raise Refused("phase findings must be an array")
   344	    for finding in findings:
   345	        if not isinstance(finding, dict) or finding.get("source") != "codex-review":
   346	            continue
   347	        value = finding.get("file")
   348	        if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
   349	            raise Refused("codex-review finding file is missing")
   350	        severity = finding.get("severity")
   351	        if not isinstance(severity, str) or severity.upper() not in PHASE4_SEVERITIES:
   352	            raise Refused("codex-review finding severity is invalid")
   353	        try:
   354	            path = normalize_finding_path(repo, value)
   355	        except ValueError:
   356	            path = None
   357	        if path is None:
   358	            unresolved += 1
   359	        else:
   360	            normalized.add((path, severity.upper()))
   361	    rank = {"CRITICAL": 4, "HIGH": 3, "MEDIUM": 2, "LOW": 1}
   362	    ordered = [{"file": path, "severity": severity}
   363	               for path, severity in sorted(normalized,
   364	                                            key=lambda item: (-rank[item[1]], item[0]))]
   365	    return PHASE4_ELIGIBILITY[key], codex, ordered, unresolved

   368	def phase4_flip_count(records, current):
   369	    if current.get("truncated"):
   370	        return 0
   371	    keys = ("worktreeDigest", "contractVersion", "configSha", "carryForwardSha")
   372	    previous = next((record for record in reversed(records)
   373	                     if not record.get("truncated")
   374	                     and all(record.get(key) == current.get(key) for key in keys)), None)
   375	    if previous is None:
   376	        return 0
   377	    old = {item["file"] for item in previous["findings"]
   378	           if item["severity"] in {"CRITICAL", "HIGH"}}
   379	    new = {item["file"] for item in current["findings"]
   380	           if item["severity"] in {"CRITICAL", "HIGH"}}
   381	    return len(old.symmetric_difference(new))
   382	
   383	
   384	def trim_phase4_runs(records):
   385	    if len(records) <= 5:
   386	        return records
   387	    latest_digest = records[-1]["worktreeDigest"]
   388	    keep_indexes = set(range(max(0, len(records) - 5), len(records)))
   389	    source_index = next((index for index in range(len(records) - 2, -1, -1)
   390	                         if records[index]["worktreeDigest"] != latest_digest), None)
   391	    if source_index is not None:
   392	        keep_indexes.add(source_index)
   393	    return [record for index, record in enumerate(records) if index in keep_indexes]

   396	def run_tree_digest(repo, manifest):
   397	    command = [sys.executable, os.path.join(HERE, "tree-digest.py"),
   398	               "--repo-root", repo, "--include-head"]
   399	    for item in manifest.get("digestExclude", []):
   400	        command.extend(["--exclude", item])
   401	    proc = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True)
   402	    if proc.returncode:
   403	        raise Refused(proc.stderr.strip() or "tree-digest failed")
   404	    return json.loads(proc.stdout)["digest"]

   407	def lock_recheck(fd, lock_path, runid, expected_inode):
   408	    try:
   409	        inode_fd = os.fstat(fd).st_ino
   410	        inode_path = os.stat(lock_path, follow_symlinks=False).st_ino
   411	    except OSError as exc:
   412	        raise Refused(f"lock disappeared or changed: {exc}") from exc
   413	    os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
   414	    raw = os.read(fd, 65536)
   415	    holder = parse_json(raw, "lock")
   416	    if inode_fd != inode_path or inode_fd != expected_inode or holder.get("runid") != runid:
   417	        raise Refused("lock inode or runid mismatch")
   418	    return holder

   421	def validate_evidence(value):
   422	    if not isinstance(value, dict):
   423	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE is not an object")
   424	    if REQUIRED_EXPECT - set(value):
   425	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE required keys are missing")
   426	    if not isinstance(value["runid"], str) or not isinstance(value["runDir"], str):
   427	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE run identity has invalid types")
   428	    if (isinstance(value["lockIno"], bool) or not isinstance(value["lockIno"], int)
   429	            or value["lockIno"] <= 0):
   430	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE lockIno has invalid type")
   640	            os.unlink(temporary)
   641	        except FileNotFoundError:
   642	            pass
   643	        except OSError as exc:
   644	            cleanup_error = exc
   645	    return linked, durability_unknown, cleanup_error

   652	def release_lock(lock_path, lock_inode):
   653	    if os.stat(lock_path, follow_symlinks=False).st_ino != lock_inode:
   654	        raise OSError("lock path no longer names the owned inode")
   655	    os.unlink(lock_path)

   658	def rewrite_lock_holder(fd, holder):
   659	    raw = json_bytes(holder)
   660	    os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
   661	    os.ftruncate(fd, 0)
   662	    write_all(fd, raw)
   663	    os.fsync(fd)

   666	def quarantine_history(history_path, runid):
   667	    if not os.path.exists(history_path):
   668	        return
   669	    target = history_path + ".tainted-" + runid
   670	    try:
   671	        os.replace(history_path, target)
   672	    except OSError as exc:
   673	        raise HistoryQuarantineFailed("history-quarantine-failed") from exc
   674	    if os.path.exists(history_path):
   675	        raise HistoryQuarantineFailed("history-quarantine-failed")
   676

   678	def taint_repo_from_run_dir(run_dir):
   679	    return os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(run_dir))))
   680

   682	def early_taint(args):
   683	    run_dir = os.path.realpath(args.run_dir)
   684	    run_base = os.path.dirname(run_dir)
   685	    lock_path = os.path.join(run_base, "lock")
   686	    repo = os.path.realpath(args.repo_root) if args.repo_root else taint_repo_from_run_dir(run_dir)
   687	    state_dir = os.path.join(repo, ".claude", "state")
   688	    history_path = os.path.join(state_dir, "docaudit-history.json")
   689	    last_run_path = os.path.join(state_dir, "docaudit-last-run.json")
   690	    lock_fd = None
   691	    try:
   692	        expected = json.loads(args.expect_json)
   693	        validate_taint_evidence(expected)
   694	        if (expected.get("runid") != args.runid
   695	                or os.path.realpath(str(expected.get("runDir"))) != run_dir
   696	                or os.path.basename(run_dir) != args.runid):
   697	            raise Refused("EVIDENCE run identity mismatch")
   698	        lock_fd = os.open(lock_path, os.O_RDWR | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
   699	        try:
   700	            fcntl.flock(lock_fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
   701	        except BlockingIOError as exc:
   702	            raise Refused("lock is held by another process") from exc
   703	        lock_inode = os.fstat(lock_fd).st_ino
   704	        path_inode = os.stat(lock_path, follow_symlinks=False).st_ino
   705	        os.lseek(lock_fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
   706	        holder = parse_json(os.read(lock_fd, 65536), "lock")
   707	        if (not isinstance(holder, dict) or holder.get("runid") != args.runid
   708	                or path_inode != lock_inode or lock_inode != expected.get("lockIno")):
   709	            raise Refused("lock inode or runid mismatch")
   710	        if args.detail is not None:
   711	            if (len(args.detail) > 200 or any(ord(char) < 32 or ord(char) == 127
   712	                                              for char in args.detail)):
   713	                raise Refused("detail is invalid")
   714	        now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()
   715	        last_state = {"runid": args.runid, "verdict": "REFUSED",
   716	                      "reason": "config-changed" if args.taint_observed == "config"
   717	                      else "history-changed",
   718	                      "observedBy": args.observed_by, "ts": now,
   719	                      "reportStatus": "not-requested"}
   720	        if args.detail is not None:
   721	            last_state["detail"] = args.detail
   722	        if args.taint_observed == "config":
   723	            last_state["expectedConfigSha"] = expected["config"]
   724	            last_state["configAcceptanceRequired"] = True
   725	            atomic(last_run_path, last_state)
   726	            release_lock(lock_path, lock_inode)
   727	            print(json.dumps({"verdict": "REFUSED", "anchorWritten": False,
   728	                              "reason": "config-changed", "observedBy": args.observed_by},
   729	                             sort_keys=True))
   730	            return 3
   731	        try:
   732	            quarantine_history(history_path, args.runid)
   733	        except HistoryQuarantineFailed:
   734	            failed_state = dict(last_state, reason="history-quarantine-failed",
   735	                                historyQuarantineFailed=True)
   736	            state_written = False
   737	            holder_written = False
   738	            try:
   739	                atomic(last_run_path, failed_state)
   740	                state_written = True
   741	            except OSError:
   742	                pass
   743	            try:
   744	                rewrite_lock_holder(lock_fd, dict(holder, historyQuarantineFailed=True))
   745	                holder_written = True
   746	            except OSError:
   747	                pass
   748	            reason = ("history-quarantine-failed" if state_written or holder_written
   749	                      else "quarantine-marker-unpersisted")
   750	            if reason == "quarantine-marker-unpersisted":
   751	                print(reason, file=sys.stderr)
   752	            print(json.dumps({"verdict": "REFUSED", "anchorWritten": False,
   753	                              "reason": reason, "observedBy": args.observed_by}, sort_keys=True))
   754	            return 3
   755	        atomic(last_run_path, last_state)
   756	        release_lock(lock_path, lock_inode)
   757	        print(json.dumps({"verdict": "REFUSED", "anchorWritten": False,
   758	                          "reason": "history-changed", "observedBy": args.observed_by},
   759	                         sort_keys=True))
   760	        return 3
   761	    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, Refused, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   762	        print(json.dumps({"verdict": "REFUSED", "anchorWritten": False,
   763	                          "reason": str(exc)}, sort_keys=True))
   764	        return 3
   765	    finally:
   766	        if lock_fd is not None:
   767	            os.close(lock_fd)
   768	
   769	
   770	def add_warning(warnings, code):
   771	    if code not in warnings:
   772	        warnings.append(code)
   773	
   774	
   775	def finalize_report(repo, run_dir, rule, report_date, last_run_path, base_state, warnings,
   776	                    verdict, *, reason=None, counts=None, history_status=None,
   777	                    sibling=None, anchor_written=False):
   778	    if rule is None:
   779	        return None, base_state["reportStatus"]
   780	    report_path = None
   781	    status = "failed"
   782	    error_code = None
   783	    try:
   784	        template = load_report_template(run_dir)
   785	        rendered = render_report(
   786	            template, verdict, report_date, reason=reason, counts=counts,
   787	            history_status=history_status, warnings=warnings, sibling=sibling,
   788	            anchor_written=anchor_written)
   789	        report_path, durability_unknown, cleanup_error = publish_report(
   790	            repo, run_dir, rule, rendered)
   900	        if expected.get("runid") != args.runid or os.path.realpath(str(expected.get("runDir"))) != run_dir:
   901	            raise Refused("EVIDENCE run identity mismatch")
   902	        lock_fd = os.open(lock_path, os.O_RDWR | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
   903	        try:
   904	            fcntl.flock(lock_fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
   905	        except BlockingIOError as exc:
   906	            raise Refused("lock is held by another process") from exc
   907	        lock_inode = os.fstat(lock_fd).st_ino
   908	        try:
   909	            path_inode = os.stat(lock_path, follow_symlinks=False).st_ino
   910	        except OSError as exc:
   911	            raise Refused(f"lock path cannot be inspected: {exc}") from exc
   912	        os.lseek(lock_fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
   913	        holder = parse_json(os.read(lock_fd, 65536), "lock")
   914	        owned = (holder.get("runid") == args.runid
   915	                 and path_inode == lock_inode
   916	                 and lock_inode == expected["lockIno"])
   917	        if path_inode != lock_inode:
   918	            raise Refused("lock was unlinked/recreated")
   919	
   920	        # Every evidence file is read exactly once into this immutable snapshot.
   921	        manifest_raw = read_once(os.path.join(run_dir, "manifest.json"), "manifest.json")
   922	        dispatch_raw = read_once(os.path.join(run_dir, "dispatch.json"), "dispatch.json")
   923	        try:
   924	            impact_raw = read_once(os.path.join(run_dir, "impact.json"), "impact.json")
   925	        except Refused as exc:
   926	            raise Refused("impact sha mismatch") from exc
   927	        returns_raw = read_once(os.path.join(run_dir, "returns.json"), "returns.json")
   928	        manifest = parse_json(manifest_raw, "manifest.json")
   929	        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
   930	            raise Refused("manifest must be an object")
   931	        verify_sha(manifest_raw, expected["manifest"], "manifest")
   932	        if manifest.get("sealed") is not True:
   933	            raise Refused("manifest is not sealed")
   934	        identity_mismatch = (holder.get("runid") != args.runid
   935	                             or manifest.get("runid") != args.runid
   936	                             or os.path.basename(run_dir) != args.runid
   937	                             or lock_inode != expected.get("lockIno"))
   938	        identity_ok = not identity_mismatch
   939	        try:
   940	            config_raw, config = load_sealed_config(config_path, expected["config"])
   941	        except SealedConfigMismatch as exc:
   942	            config_taint = True
   943	            raise Refused(str(exc)) from exc
   944	        config_signature = state_signature(config_path)
   945	        report_rule = validate_report_rule(manifest, repo, args.runid)
   946	        verify_sha(dispatch_raw, expected["dispatch"], "dispatch")
   947	        verify_sha(returns_raw, expected["returns"], "returns")
   948	        dispatch_doc = parse_json(dispatch_raw, "dispatch.json")
   949	        if not isinstance(dispatch_doc, dict) or sha256_bytes(impact_raw) != dispatch_doc.get("impactSha"):
   950	            raise Refused("impact sha mismatch")
   951	        impact_doc = parse_json(impact_raw, "impact.json")
   952	        returns = parse_json(returns_raw, "returns.json")
   953	        if not all(isinstance(value, dict) for value in (manifest, dispatch_doc, config)):
   954	            raise Refused("manifest, dispatch, and config must be objects")
   955	        codex_review_config = config.get("codexReview", {})
   956	        if not isinstance(codex_review_config, dict):
   957	            codex_review_config = {}
   958	        codex_review_required = codex_review_config.get("required", False)
   959	        if not isinstance(codex_review_required, bool):
   960	            raise Refused("codexReview.required must be boolean")
   961	        codex_review_status["required"] = codex_review_required
   962	        if codex_review_required and codex_review_config.get("enabled") is False:
   963	            raise Refused("codexReview.required conflicts with enabled:false")
   964	        backend = manifest.get("phase3Backend")
   965	        if (not isinstance(backend, str) or backend not in VALID_BACKENDS
   966	                or backend != config.get("phase3Backend", "workflow")):
   967	            raise Refused("sealed phase3Backend is invalid or does not match config")
   968	        report_trusted = identity_ok
   969	        validate_returns(returns)
   970	        if expected.get("attempt") != max((item["attempt"] for item in returns), default=0):
   971	            raise Refused("EVIDENCE attempt does not match returns")
   972	        history_entries = []
   973	        phase4_runs = []
   974	        history_raw = None
   975	        if expected["history"] == "none":
   976	            if os.path.exists(history_path) or expected.get("historyStatus") != "absent":
   977	                history_taint = os.path.exists(history_path)
   978	                raise Refused("history=none sentinel is invalid")
   979	        else:
   980	            history_raw, history_signature = read_state_once(history_path, "history")
   981	            if sha256_bytes(history_raw) != expected["history"]:
   982	                history_taint = True
   983	                raise Refused("history sha mismatch")
   984	            if expected.get("historyStatus") == "ok":
   985	                try:
   986	                    history_entries, phase4_runs, history_warnings = parse_history_document(
   987	                        parse_json(history_raw, "history"))
   988	                    for warning in history_warnings:
   989	                        add_warning(warnings, warning)
   990	                except (ValueError, Refused) as exc:
   991	                    history_taint = True
   992	                    raise Refused(f"history is corrupt: {exc}") from exc
   993	            elif expected.get("historyStatus") == "corrupt":
   994	                history_taint = True
   995	            else:
   996	                raise Refused("historyStatus is invalid")
   997	
   998	        if expected["anchor"] == "none":
   999	            if os.path.exists(anchor_path):
  1000	                anchor_taint = True
  1001	                raise Refused("anchor=none sentinel is invalid")
  1002	        else:
  1003	            anchor_raw, anchor_signature = read_state_once(anchor_path, "anchor")
  1004	            if sha256_bytes(anchor_raw) != expected["anchor"]:
  1005	                anchor_taint = True
  1006	                raise Refused("anchor sha mismatch")
  1007	
  1008	        if identity_mismatch:
  1009	            raise Refused("lock/manifest/run directory identity mismatch")
  1010	
  1011	        preflight = None
  1012	        preflight_path = os.path.join(run_dir, "preflight.json")
  1013	        if expected["preflight"] == "none":
  1014	            if manifest.get("preflightRequired") or os.path.exists(preflight_path):
  1015	                raise Refused("preflight=none sentinel is invalid")
  1016	        else:
  1017	            raw = read_once(preflight_path, "preflight.json")
  1018	            verify_sha(raw, expected["preflight"], "preflight")
  1019	            preflight = parse_json(raw, "preflight.json")
  1020	
  1021	        phase4 = None
  1022	        phase4_eligible = False
  1023	        phase4_findings = []
  1024	        phase4_unresolved = 0
  1025	        codex_review_evidence = None
  1026	        phase4_path = os.path.join(run_dir, "phase4.json")
  1027	        if expected["phase4"] == "none":
  1028	            if os.path.exists(phase4_path):
  1029	                raise Refused("phase4=none sentinel is invalid")
  1030	            if codex_review_required:
  1031	                raise Refused("codex-review required but state=missing")
  1032	            if manifest.get("phase4Required"):
  1033	                raise Refused("phase4=none sentinel is invalid")
  1034	        else:
  1035	            raw = read_once(phase4_path, "phase4.json")
  1036	            verify_sha(raw, expected["phase4"], "phase4")
  1037	            phase4 = parse_json(raw, "phase4.json")
  1038	            (phase4_eligible, codex_review_evidence, phase4_findings,
  1039	             phase4_unresolved) = validate_phase4_contract(repo, manifest, phase4)
  1040	
  1041	        codex_review_state = (codex_review_evidence.get("state")
  1042	                              if codex_review_evidence is not None else None)
  1043	        codex_review_status["state"] = codex_review_state
  1044	        if codex_review_required and codex_review_state != "completed":
  1045	            raise Refused(f"codex-review required but state={codex_review_state or 'missing'}")
  1046	
  1047	        head = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD"], capture_output=True,
  1048	                              text=True, check=True).stdout.strip()
  1049	        if head != manifest.get("head"):
  1050	            raise Refused("HEAD does not match sealed manifest")
  1051	        digest = run_tree_digest(repo, manifest)
  1052	        if digest != manifest.get("worktreeDigest") or digest != expected.get("digest"):
  1053	            verify_audit_scope_at_barrier(repo, config, manifest.get("auditScopeSha"))
  1054	            raise Refused("worktree digest mismatch")
  1055	        change_proc = subprocess.run(
  1056	            [sys.executable, os.path.join(HERE, "change-set-sha.py"), "--repo-root", repo,
  1057	             "--baseline-sha", manifest["baselineSha"], "--config", config_path,
  1058	             "--expect-config-sha", expected["config"]],
  1059	            capture_output=True, text=True)
  1060	        if change_proc.returncode == 7:
  1061	            config_taint = True
  1062	            raise Refused(change_proc.stderr.strip() or "sealed-config-mismatch")
  1063	        if change_proc.returncode:
  1064	            raise Refused(change_proc.stderr.strip() or "change-set-sha failed")
  1065	        if json.loads(change_proc.stdout).get("changeSetSha") != manifest.get("changeSetSha"):
  1066	            raise Refused("changeSetSha mismatch")
  1067	
  1068	        impacted = manifest.get("impacted")
  1069	        dispatched = manifest.get("dispatch")
  1070	        cached = manifest.get("cached")
  1071	        if not all(isinstance(value, list) for value in (impacted, dispatched, cached)):
  1072	            raise Refused("manifest path sets are invalid")
  1073	        if (manifest.get("mode") == "full" and not impacted
  1074	                and not manifest.get("emptyCorpus")):
  1075	            raise Refused("full mode requires impacted documents unless the corpus is empty")
  1076	        if set(dispatched) | set(cached) != set(impacted) or set(dispatched) & set(cached):
  1077	            raise Refused("manifest dispatch/cached partition is invalid")
  1078	        validate_provenance(manifest, impacted, impact_provenance(impact_doc))
  1079	        for field in ("dispatch", "cached", "changeSetSha", "changedSet", "baselineSha",
  1080	                      "contractVersion", "historyStatus"):
  1081	            if dispatch_doc.get(field) != manifest.get(field) and field not in {"historyStatus"}:
  1082	                raise Refused(f"manifest and dispatch.json differ at {field}")
  1083	        if dispatch_doc.get("historyStatus") != expected.get("historyStatus"):
  1084	            raise Refused("dispatch historyStatus mismatch")
  1085	
  1086	        verdict_dir = os.path.join(run_dir, "verdicts")
  1087	        verdicts = {}
  1088	        verdict_raw = {}
  1089	        for name in sorted(os.listdir(verdict_dir)) if os.path.isdir(verdict_dir) else []:
  1090	            if not name.endswith(".json"):
  1091	                continue
  1092	            raw = read_once(os.path.join(verdict_dir, name), f"verdicts/{name}")
  1093	            record = parse_json(raw, f"verdicts/{name}")
  1094	            path = record.get("path") if isinstance(record, dict) else None
  1095	            if not isinstance(path, str) or path in verdicts:
  1096	                raise Refused("verdict path is invalid or duplicated")
  1097	            if record.get("runid") != args.runid or record.get("verdict") not in VALID_VERDICTS:
  1098	                raise Refused("verdict runid/value is invalid")
  1099	            verdicts[path] = record
  1100	            verdict_raw[path] = raw
  1101	        if set(verdicts) != set(impacted):
  1102	            raise Refused("verdict set does not equal impacted set")
  1103	        cached_material = b"".join(verdict_raw[path] for path in sorted(cached))
  1104	        if cached:
  1105	            if expected["cached"] == "none" or sha256_bytes(cached_material) != expected["cached"]:
  1106	                raise Refused("cached verdict aggregate sha mismatch")
  1107	        elif expected["cached"] != "none":
  1108	            raise Refused("cached sentinel is invalid")
  1109	
  1110	        final_returns = {}
  1111	        for item in returns:
  1112	            path = item["assignedPath"]
  1113	            if path in dispatched and (path not in final_returns or item["attempt"] > final_returns[path]["attempt"]):
  1114	                final_returns[path] = item
  1115	        for path in dispatched:
  1116	            item = final_returns.get(path)
  1117	            if (item is None or item.get("returnedPath") != path
  1118	                    or item.get("verdict") != verdicts[path]["verdict"]):
  1119	                raise Refused(f"final return mismatch for {path}")
  1120	
  1120	
  1121	        enabled, minimum, cache_warnings = validate_min_passes(config)
  1122	        for warning in cache_warnings:
  1123	            add_warning(warnings, warning)
  1124	        codex_review_status["degraded"] = (
  1125	            not codex_review_required
  1126	            and codex_review_state in {"execution-failed", "ref-invalid"})
  1127	        if codex_review_status["degraded"]:
  1128	            add_warning(
  1129	                warnings,
  1130	                f"codex-review did not run ({codex_review_state}) — verdict excludes the adversarial layer")
  1131	        if cached and (not enabled or expected.get("historyStatus") != "ok"):
  1132	            raise Refused("cached verdicts are not permitted")
  1133	        for path in cached:
  1134	            record = verdicts[path]
  1135	            if record.get("source") != "cache":
  1136	                raise Refused(f"cached verdict source mismatch for {path}")
  1137	            current_sha = content_sha(repo, path)
  1138	            ok, runids, _reason = cache_qualification(
  1139	                history_entries, path, current_sha, manifest["changeSetSha"],
  1140	                manifest["contractVersion"], minimum or 2, backend)
  1141	            if (not ok or record.get("contentSha") != current_sha
  1142	                    or record.get("changeSetSha") != manifest["changeSetSha"]
  1143	                    or record.get("contractVersion") != manifest["contractVersion"]
  1144	                    or record.get("historyRunids") != runids):
  1145	                raise Refused(f"cached verdict is not history-qualified for {path}")
  1146	
  1147	        has_fail = any(record["verdict"] == "FAIL" for record in verdicts.values())
  1148	        if preflight is not None:
  1149	            has_fail = findings_fail(preflight) or has_fail
  1150	        if phase4 is not None:
  1151	            has_fail = findings_fail(phase4) or has_fail
  1152	        verdict = "NEEDS_FIX" if has_fail else "CONSISTENT"
  1153	
  1154	        # The sibling scan remains inside the lock and precedes the indivisible barrier.
  1155	        sibling = run_sibling_step(manifest, returns, phase4, config, repo)
  1156	
  1157	        # Recheck every mutable root as one barrier immediately before state commit.
  1158	        lock_recheck(lock_fd, lock_path, args.runid, expected["lockIno"])
  1159	        head2 = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD"], capture_output=True,
  1160	                               text=True, check=True).stdout.strip()
  1161	        if head2 != head or run_tree_digest(repo, manifest) != digest:
  1162	            verify_audit_scope_at_barrier(repo, config, manifest.get("auditScopeSha"))
  1163	            raise Refused("HEAD/worktree changed before state write")
  1164	        if not state_unchanged(history_path, history_signature):
  1165	            history_taint = True
  1166	            raise Refused("history changed before state write")
  1167	        if not state_unchanged(anchor_path, anchor_signature):
  1168	            anchor_taint = True
  1169	            raise Refused("anchor changed before state write")
  1170	        if not state_unchanged(config_path, config_signature):
  1171	            config_taint = True
  1172	            raise Refused("config changed before state write")
  1173	        verify_audit_scope_at_barrier(repo, config, manifest.get("auditScopeSha"))
  1174	        if history_taint and os.path.exists(history_path):
  1175	            quarantine_history(history_path, args.runid)
  1176	            history_entries = []
  1177	            phase4_runs = []
  1178	            history_taint = False
  1179	        now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()
  1180	        existing_keys = {(item["runid"], item["path"]) for item in history_entries}
  1181	        additions = []
  1182	        for path in impacted:
  1183	            if (args.runid, path) in existing_keys:
  1184	                raise Refused("history would duplicate (runid,path)")
  1185	            additions.append({"runid": args.runid, "path": path,
  1186	                              "contentSha": content_sha(repo, path),
  1187	                              "changeSetSha": manifest["changeSetSha"],
  1188	                              "contractVersion": manifest["contractVersion"],
  1189	                              "backend": backend,
  1190	                              "verdict": verdicts[path]["verdict"], "ts": now})
  1191	        flips, same_change_set_flips = verdict_flip_counts(
  1192	            history_entries, additions, dispatched)
  1193	        if flips:
  1194	            add_warning(
  1195	                warnings,
  1196	                f'verdict instability: {flips} document(s) changed verdict with unchanged '
  1197	                f'content since the previous run ({same_change_set_flips} with an unchanged '
  1198	                'change set) — single-pass verification samples the defect pool; "fix these '
  1199	                f'{flips} and re-run" is not guaranteed to converge (see ADOPTION)')
  1200	        phase4_flips = 0
  1201	        next_phase4_runs = list(phase4_runs)
  1202	        if phase4_eligible:
  1203	            truncated = len(phase4_findings) > 500
  1204	            current_phase4 = {
  1205	                "runid": args.runid,
  1206	                "ts": now,
  1207	                "worktreeDigest": manifest["worktreeDigest"],
  1208	                "contractVersion": manifest["contractVersion"],
  1209	                "configSha": expected["config"],
  1210	                "carryForwardSha": codex_review_evidence["carryForwardSha"],
  1211	                "unresolvedFileCount": phase4_unresolved,
  1212	                "truncated": truncated,
  1213	                "findings": phase4_findings[:500],
  1214	            }
  1215	            if phase4_unresolved:
  1216	                add_warning(warnings, f"Phase-4 unresolved finding paths: {phase4_unresolved}")
  1217	            if truncated:
  1218	                add_warning(warnings, "Phase-4 findings were truncated; flip comparison skipped")
  1219	            else:
  1220	                phase4_flips = phase4_flip_count(phase4_runs, current_phase4)
  1221	            if phase4_flips:
  1222	                add_warning(
  1223	                    warnings,
  1224	                    f'Phase-4 instability: {phase4_flips} file(s) changed blocking status with '
  1225	                    'unchanged worktree, contract, config, and carry-forward inputs — the '
  1226	                    'codex full review samples '
  1227	                    'the defect pool; "fix N and re-run" is not guaranteed to converge '
  1228	                    '(see ADOPTION)')
  1229	            candidate_runs = trim_phase4_runs(phase4_runs + [current_phase4])
  1230	            candidate_doc = {"entries": trim_history(history_entries + additions),
  1231	                             "phase4Runs": candidate_runs}
  1232	            try:
  1233	                _entries_check, _runs_check, roundtrip_warnings = parse_history_document(
  1234	                    json.loads(json.dumps(candidate_doc, ensure_ascii=True)))
  1235	                if roundtrip_warnings:
  1236	                    raise ValueError("phase4Runs round-trip degraded")
  1237	                next_phase4_runs = candidate_runs
  1238	            except ValueError:
  1239	                add_warning(warnings, "Phase-4 history record failed round-trip validation")
  1240	                phase4_flips = 0
  1241	        atomic(history_path, {"entries": trim_history(history_entries + additions),
  1242	                              "phase4Runs": next_phase4_runs})
  1243	        report_status = "pending" if report_rule is not None else "not-requested"
  1244	        last_state = {"runid": args.runid, "verdict": verdict, "ts": now,
  1245	                      "reportStatus": report_status}
  1246	        atomic(last_run_path, last_state)
  1247	        anchor_written = False
  1248	        if verdict == "CONSISTENT":
  1249	            atomic(anchor_path, {"sha": head, "head": head, "digest": digest,
  1250	                                 "runid": args.runid,
  1251	                                 "contractVersion": manifest["contractVersion"]})
  1252	            anchor_written = True
  1253	        counts = {"impacted": len(impacted), "dispatch": len(dispatched),
  1254	                  "cached": len(cached),
  1255	                  "verdictFlipsUnchangedContent": flips,
  1256	                  "verdictFlipsUnchangedContentSameChangeSet": same_change_set_flips,
  1257	                  "phase4FlipsUnchangedContent": phase4_flips}
  1258	        report_verdict = verdict
  1259	        if verdict == "CONSISTENT" and codex_review_status["degraded"]:
  1260	            report_verdict = f"CONSISTENT (codex-review did not run: {codex_review_state})"
  1261	        report_path, report_status = finalize_report(
  1262	            repo, run_dir, report_rule, manifest["reportDate"], last_run_path,
  1263	            last_state, warnings, report_verdict, counts=counts,
  1264	            history_status=expected["historyStatus"], sibling=sibling,
  1265	            anchor_written=anchor_written)
  1266	        try:
  1267	            release_lock(lock_path, lock_inode)
  1268	        except OSError:
  1269	            add_warning(warnings, "lockReleaseFailed")
  1270	        os.close(lock_fd)
  1271	        lock_fd = None
  1272	        result = {"verdict": verdict, "anchorWritten": anchor_written,
  1273	                  "runid": args.runid, "counts": counts,
  1274	                  "historyStatus": expected["historyStatus"], "warnings": warnings,
  1275	                  "siblingScan": sibling, "reportStatus": report_status,
  1276	                  "codexReview": codex_review_status}
  1277	        if report_path is not None:
  1278	            result["reportPath"] = report_path
  1279	        print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True))
  1280	        return 0
  1281	    except (Refused, HistoryQuarantineFailed, OSError, ValueError, KeyError, json.JSONDecodeError,
  1282	            subprocess.CalledProcessError) as exc:
  1283	        reason = str(exc)
  1284	        # Degrade only state owned by this run; never touch a later run's state.
  1285	        report_path = None
  1286	        report_status = None
  1287	        if owned:
  1288	            quarantine_failed = False

  1291	                    quarantine_history(history_path, args.runid)
  1292	                    history_taint = False
  1293	            except HistoryQuarantineFailed:
  1294	                quarantine_failed = True

  1296	                if anchor_taint and os.path.exists(anchor_path):
  1297	                    os.unlink(anchor_path)
  1298	            except OSError:
  1299	                pass
  1300	            can_report = (report_trusted and report_rule is not None and not config_taint
  1301	                          and not quarantine_failed)
  1302	            state_written = False
  1303	            holder_marker_written = False
  1304	            if identity_ok:
  1305	                now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()
  1306	                recorded_reason = ("history-quarantine-failed" if quarantine_failed
  1307	                                   else "config-changed" if config_taint else reason)
  1308	                last_state = {"runid": args.runid, "verdict": "REFUSED",
  1309	                              "reason": recorded_reason, "ts": now,
  1310	                              "reportStatus": "pending" if can_report else "not-requested"}
  1311	                if config_taint:
  1312	                    last_state["expectedConfigSha"] = expected.get("config")
  1313	                    last_state["configAcceptanceRequired"] = True
  1314	                if quarantine_failed:
  1315	                    last_state["historyQuarantineFailed"] = True
  1316	                try:
  1317	                    atomic(last_run_path, last_state)
  1318	                    state_written = True
  1319	                    report_status = last_state["reportStatus"]
  1320	                except OSError:
  1321	                    add_warning(warnings, "reportStatusUpdateFailed")
  1322	                if quarantine_failed and lock_fd is not None:
  1323	                    try:
  1324	                        rewrite_lock_holder(
  1325	                            lock_fd, dict(holder, historyQuarantineFailed=True))
  1326	                        holder_marker_written = True
  1327	                    except OSError:
  1328	                        pass
  1329	                    if not state_written and not holder_marker_written:
  1330	                        reason = "quarantine-marker-unpersisted"
  1331	                        print(reason, file=sys.stderr)
  1332	                if can_report and state_written:
  1333	                    report_path, report_status = finalize_report(
  1334	                        repo, run_dir, report_rule, manifest["reportDate"], last_run_path,
  1335	                        last_state, warnings, "REFUSED", reason=reason,
  1336	                        anchor_written=False)
  1337	            if lock_fd is not None and not quarantine_failed:
  1338	                try:
  1339	                    release_lock(lock_path, lock_inode)
  1340	                except OSError:
  1341	                    add_warning(warnings, "lockReleaseFailed")
  1342	        output_reason = "config-changed" if config_taint else reason
  1343	        result = {"verdict": "REFUSED", "anchorWritten": False, "reason": output_reason,
  1344	                  "codexReview": codex_review_status}

  1348	            result["reportStatus"] = report_status
  1349	        if report_path is not None:
  1350	            result["reportPath"] = report_path
  1351	        print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True))
  1352	        return 3
  1353	    finally:
  1354	        if lock_fd is not None:
  1355	            os.close(lock_fd)
  1356

  1358	if __name__ == "__main__":
  1359	    sys.exit(main())

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Shared deterministic history/cache rules for plan-dispatch and the gate."""
     3	
     4	import hashlib
     5	import json
     6	import os
     7	import re
     8	import unicodedata
     9	
    10	
    11	VALID_VERDICTS = {"PASS", "WARN", "FAIL"}
    12	VALID_BACKENDS = {"workflow", "codex"}
    13	CODEX_REVIEW_STATES = ("completed", "execution-failed", "ref-invalid",
    14	                       "skipped-full-run", "not-active")
    15	HISTORY_FIELDS = {
    16	    "runid": str,
    17	    "path": str,
    18	    "contentSha": str,
    19	    "changeSetSha": str,
    20	    "contractVersion": str,
    21	    "verdict": str,
    22	    "ts": str,
    23	}
    24	PHASE4_SEVERITIES = {"CRITICAL", "HIGH", "MEDIUM", "LOW"}
    25	PHASE4_RUN_FIELDS = {
    26	    "runid": str,
    27	    "ts": str,
    28	    "worktreeDigest": str,
    29	    "contractVersion": str,
    30	    "configSha": str,
    31	    "carryForwardSha": str,
    32	    "unresolvedFileCount": int,
    33	    "truncated": bool,
    34	    "findings": list,
    35	}
    36	PHASE4_RUN_LIMIT = 6
    37	PHASE4_FINDING_LIMIT = 500
    38	PHASE4_FILE_BYTES = 512
    39	PHASE4_RECORD_BYTES = 512 * 1024
    40	PHASE4_RUNS_BYTES = 1024 * 1024
    41	
    42	
    43	def sha256_bytes(data):
    44	    return "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    45	
    46	
    47	def content_sha(repo_root, path):
    48	    with open(os.path.join(repo_root, *path.split("/")), "rb") as handle:
    49	        return sha256_bytes(handle.read())
    50	
    51	
    52	def validate_min_passes(config):
    53	    cache = config.get("verdictCache", {})
    54	    enabled = cache.get("enabled", True)
    55	    value = cache.get("minConsecutivePasses", 2)
    56	    if not enabled:
    57	        return False, None, []
    58	    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int) or not 2 <= value <= 10:
    59	        return False, None, ["verdictCache.minConsecutivePasses must be an integer from 2 through 10; cache disabled"]
    60	    return True, value, []
    61	
    62	
    63	def parse_history(data):
    64	    if isinstance(data, dict):
    65	        entries = data.get("entries")
    66	    else:
    67	        entries = data
    68	    if not isinstance(entries, list):
    69	        raise ValueError("history entries must be a list")
    70	    seen = set()
    71	    for index, entry in enumerate(entries):
    72	        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    73	            raise ValueError(f"history entry {index} is not an object")
    74	        for field, typ in HISTORY_FIELDS.items():
    75	            if not isinstance(entry.get(field), typ):
    76	                raise ValueError(f"history entry {index} has invalid {field}")
    77	        if entry["verdict"] not in VALID_VERDICTS:
    78	            raise ValueError(f"history entry {index} has invalid verdict")
    79	        backend = entry.get("backend", "workflow")
    80	        if not isinstance(backend, str) or backend not in VALID_BACKENDS:
    81	            raise ValueError(f"history entry {index} has invalid backend")
    82	        key = (entry["runid"], entry["path"])
    83	        if key in seen:
    84	            raise ValueError("history contains duplicate (runid,path)")
    85	        seen.add(key)
    86	    return entries
    87	
    88	
    89	def _canonical_size(value):
    90	    return len(json.dumps(
    91	        value, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True
    92	    ).encode("utf-8"))
    93	
    94	
    95	def _validate_phase4_path(path):
    96	    if not isinstance(path, str) or not path:
    97	        raise ValueError("finding file must be a non-empty string")
    98	    if (path.startswith(("./", "/", "//"))
    99	            or re.match(r"^[A-Za-z]:", path)
   100	            or "\\" in path or '"' in path
   101	            or any(unicodedata.category(char) == "Cc" for char in path)):
   102	        raise ValueError("finding file is not a canonical repository path")
   103	    if any(part in ("", ".", "..") for part in path.split("/")):
   104	        raise ValueError("finding file is not a canonical repository path")
   105	    if _canonical_size(path) > PHASE4_FILE_BYTES:
   106	        raise ValueError("finding file exceeds 512 serialized bytes")
   107	
   108	
   109	def _validate_phase4_runs(phase4_runs):
   110	    if not isinstance(phase4_runs, list):
   111	        raise ValueError("phase4Runs must be a list")
   112	    if len(phase4_runs) > PHASE4_RUN_LIMIT:
   113	        raise ValueError("phase4Runs exceeds 6 records")
   114	    if _canonical_size(phase4_runs) > PHASE4_RUNS_BYTES:
   115	        raise ValueError("phase4Runs exceeds 1 MiB")
   116	    for record_index, record in enumerate(phase4_runs):
   117	        if not isinstance(record, dict):
   118	            raise ValueError(f"phase4Runs record {record_index} is not an object")
   119	        for field, expected_type in PHASE4_RUN_FIELDS.items():
   120	            value = record.get(field)
   121	            if expected_type is int:
   122	                valid = isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)
   123	            else:
   124	                valid = isinstance(value, expected_type)
   125	            if not valid:
   126	                raise ValueError(
   127	                    f"phase4Runs record {record_index} has invalid {field}")
   128	        if _canonical_size(record) > PHASE4_RECORD_BYTES:
   129	            raise ValueError(
   130	                f"phase4Runs record {record_index} exceeds 512 KiB")
   131	        findings = record["findings"]
   132	        if len(findings) > PHASE4_FINDING_LIMIT:
   133	            raise ValueError(
   134	                f"phase4Runs record {record_index} exceeds 500 findings")
   135	        for finding_index, finding in enumerate(findings):
   136	            if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   137	                raise ValueError(
   138	                    f"phase4Runs record {record_index} finding {finding_index} is not an object")
   139	            _validate_phase4_path(finding.get("file"))
   140	            if finding.get("severity") not in PHASE4_SEVERITIES:
   141	                raise ValueError(
   142	                    f"phase4Runs record {record_index} finding {finding_index} has invalid severity")
   143	
   144	
   145	def parse_history_document(data):
   146	    """Return ``(entries, phase4_runs, warnings)`` for every valid history.
   147	
   148	    Invalid deterministic entries remain a corrupt-history error.  Invalid
   149	    Phase-4 sampling records degrade independently so deterministic cache data
   150	    remains usable.
   151	    """
   152	    entries = parse_history(data)
   153	    if isinstance(data, list) or "phase4Runs" not in data:
   154	        return entries, [], []
   155	    phase4_runs = data["phase4Runs"]
   156	    try:
   157	        _validate_phase4_runs(phase4_runs)
   158	    except ValueError as exc:
   159	        return entries, [], [f"phase4Runs ignored: {exc}"]
   160	    return entries, phase4_runs, []
   161	
   162	
   163	def cache_qualification(entries, path, current_content_sha, change_set_sha,
   164	                        contract_version, minimum, backend="workflow"):
   165	    if not isinstance(backend, str) or backend not in VALID_BACKENDS:
   166	        return False, [], "backend-invalid"
   167	    recent = [entry for entry in entries if entry["path"] == path][-minimum:]
   168	    if len(recent) != minimum:
   169	        return False, [], "history-insufficient"
   170	    if len({entry["runid"] for entry in recent}) != minimum:
   171	        return False, [], "history-runid-duplicate"
   172	    expected = (current_content_sha, change_set_sha, contract_version, backend)
   173	    for entry in recent:
   174	        actual = (entry["contentSha"], entry["changeSetSha"], entry["contractVersion"],
   175	                  entry.get("backend", "workflow"))
   176	        if entry["verdict"] != "PASS" or actual != expected:
   177	            return False, [], "history-key-mismatch"
   178	    return True, [entry["runid"] for entry in recent], None
   179	
   180	
   181	def trim_history(entries, per_path=20):
   182	    paths = sorted({entry["path"] for entry in entries})
   183	    kept = []
   184	    for path in paths:
   185	        kept.extend([entry for entry in entries if entry["path"] == path][-per_path:])
   186	    return kept
   187	
   188	
   189	def json_bytes(value):
   190	    return (json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, indent=2) + "\n").encode("utf-8")

   121	def atomic(path, value):
   122	    raw = json_bytes(value)
   123	    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
   124	    fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".docaudit-state.", dir=os.path.dirname(path))
   125	    try:
   126	        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as handle:
   127	            handle.write(raw)
   128	            handle.flush()
   129	            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
   130	        os.replace(temporary, path)
   131	    finally:
   132	        if os.path.exists(temporary):
   133	            os.unlink(temporary)

   152	def read_state_once(path, label):
   153	    try:
   154	        fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
   155	        try:
   156	            chunks = []
   157	            while True:
   158	                chunk = os.read(fd, 65536)
   159	                if not chunk:
   160	                    break
   161	                chunks.append(chunk)
   162	            info = os.fstat(fd)
   163	            if os.stat(path, follow_symlinks=False).st_ino != info.st_ino:
   164	                raise Refused(f"{label} was replaced while being read")
   165	            signature = (info.st_ino, info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns)
   166	            return b"".join(chunks), signature
   167	        finally:
   168	            os.close(fd)
   169	    except OSError as exc:
   170	        raise Refused(f"{label} cannot be read: {exc}") from exc

    29	def load_sealed_config(path, expected_sha):
    30	    """Return ``(raw_bytes, parsed_json)`` after a one-read SHA check."""
    31	    if not isinstance(expected_sha, str) or not SHA_RE.fullmatch(expected_sha):
    32	        raise ValueError("expected config sha must be sha256:<64 lowercase hex>")
    33	    fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
    34	    try:
    35	        chunks = []
    36	        while True:
    37	            chunk = os.read(fd, 1024 * 1024)
    38	            if not chunk:
    39	                break
    40	            chunks.append(chunk)
    41	    finally:
    42	        os.close(fd)
    43	    raw = b"".join(chunks)
    44	    observed_sha = "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
    45	    if observed_sha != expected_sha:
    46	        raise SealedConfigMismatch(expected_sha, observed_sha)
    47	    return raw, json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
    48

    50	def main():
    51	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    52	    parser.add_argument("--run-dir", required=True)
    53	    parser.add_argument("--runid", required=True)
    54	    parser.add_argument("--repo-root", required=True)
    55	    parser.add_argument("--config", required=True)
    56	    parser.add_argument("--expect-config-sha", required=True)
    57	    parser.add_argument("--history", required=True)
    58	    parser.add_argument("--impact-json", required=True)
    59	    parser.add_argument("--baseline-sha", required=True)
    60	    parser.add_argument("--mode", choices=["incremental", "full"], default="incremental")
    61	    parser.add_argument("--full", action="store_true",
    62	                        help="force full mode (alias for callers propagating /audit --full)")
    63	    parser.add_argument("--contract-version", required=True)
    64	    parser.add_argument("--evidence", required=True)
    65	    args = parser.parse_args()
    66	    try:
    67	        evidence = json.loads(args.evidence)
    68	        if (not isinstance(evidence, dict) or evidence.get("runid") != args.runid
    69	                or os.path.realpath(str(evidence.get("runDir"))) != os.path.realpath(args.run_dir)):
    70	            raise ValueError("--evidence run identity mismatch")
    71	        if args.expect_config_sha != evidence.get("config"):
    72	            raise ValueError("--expect-config-sha does not match evidence config")
    73	        _config_raw, config = load_sealed_config(
    74	            args.config, args.expect_config_sha)

    77	            impact_raw = handle.read()
    78	        impact = json.loads(impact_raw.decode("utf-8"))
    79	        impacted = paths_from_impact(impact)
    80	        mode = "full" if args.full else args.mode
